Native26 showed that Pause/Recent observations leave outer More OPEN;27 showed Escape from portaled diagnostics also leaves it OPEN. React events follow the React owner tree rather than the slot's DOM ancestry ([official createPortal reference](https://react.dev/reference/react-dom/createPortal)); the prototype's slot onClick/outer onKeyDown therefore missed those controls. Replaced those synthetic slot handlers with scoped native DOM click/keyboard boundaries. Open child navigation retains its own first Escape; a subsequent Escape closes More. A layout effect returns focus to More ONLY while hidden content still owns it; an owner's already-focused reader/decision is preserved. No action/command is duplicated or authorized by the menu.
